<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Calculate the missing angle in this isosceles triangle.

![papers/aldenham-school/2019/sample-paper-1/s01-q09-i00.png](/assets/papers/aldenham-school/2019/sample-paper-1/s01-q09-i00.png "papers/aldenham-school/2019/sample-paper-1/s01-q09-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Calculate the missing angle in this isosceles triangle.
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the missing angle $= a$

As the triangle is an isosceles triangle, the third angle $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a  + a + 96                             &= 180   \\\\
2a + 96                                 &= 180  \\\\
2a                                      &= 180 - 96  \\\\
2a                                      &= 84  \\\\
a                                       &= \dfrac {84} {2}  \\\\
a                                       &= \dfrac {42 \times 2} {2}  \\\\
a                                       &= \dfrac {42 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}}   \\\\
a                                       &= 42  \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Missing angle $= 42  \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$42 \degree$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

What is the value of the missing angle?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<div class="workings">

<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Angles on a straight line add up to} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the missing angle $= a$

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 90  + 23             &= 180  \\\\
a                        &= 180 - 90 - 23 \\\\
a                        &= 67 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Missing angle $= 67 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$67 \degree$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

![papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q15-i00.png](/assets/papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q15-i00.png "papers/aldenham-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q15-i00.png")

Estimate the size of angle $r$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<emptyline>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Angles around a point add up to} = 360 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

The acute angle is approximately $60 \degree$.

Using that we get,

$
\begin{aligned}
r + 60      &= 360 \\\\
r           &= 360 - 60 \\\\
            &= 300 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$300 \degree$

</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class="papername">
<p>alderley-edge-school-for-girls--11-plus--maths--9999--sample-paper-2:1:22</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>Waffles Woof</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>rag_wf_ng1</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Two angles of a triangle are $49 \degree$ and $52 \degree$. What is the third angle?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Two angles of a triangle are $49 \degree$ and $52 \degree$. What is the third angle?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the third angle $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
49 + 52 + a    &= 180 \\\\
101 + a        &= 180 \\\\
a              &= 180 - 101 \\\\
a              &= 79 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Third angle $= 79 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$79 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Look at triangle $ABC$.

$ABD$ is an **isosceles** triangle where $AB = AD$.

![papers/alderley-edge-school-for-girls/sample/sample-paper-2/s03-q08-i00.png](/assets/papers/alderley-edge-school-for-girls/sample/sample-paper-2/s03-q08-i00.png "papers/alderley-edge-school-for-girls/sample/sample-paper-2/s03-q08-i00.png")

Work out the sizes of angles $x, y$ and $z$.

Give reasons for your answers.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Look at triangle $ABC$.
<emptyline>
$ABD$ is an **isosceles** triangle where $AB = AD$.
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
Work out the sizes of angles $x, y$ and $z$.
<emptyline>
Give reasons for your answers.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$ABD$ is an **isosceles** triangle. So,

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
x                                       &= 74 \degree \\\\
x  + y + 74                             &= 180 \\\\
74 + y + 74                             &= 180 \\\\
y + 148                                 &= 180 \\\\
y                                       &= 180 - 148 \\\\
y                                       &= 32 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Let's use the value of $x$ and $y$ in triangle ABC.

$
\begin{aligned}
x + y + z + 28                              &= 180 \\\\
74 + 32 + z + 28                            &= 180 \\\\
z + 134                                     &= 180 \\\\
z                                           &= 180 - 134 \\\\
z                                           &= 46 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$x = 74 \degree$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$y = 32 \degree$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$z = 46 \degree$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

One of the angles in an isosceles triangle is $30 \degree$. Which of these is a possible other angle in the triangle:

-  $90 \degree$
-  $80 \degree$
-  $150 \degree$ 
-  $60 \degree$ 
-  $75 \degree$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">One of the angles in an isosceles triangle is $30 \degree$. Which of these is a possible other angle in the triangle:
<emptyline>
-  $90 \degree$
-  $80 \degree$
-  $150 \degree$ 
-  $60 \degree$ 
-  $75 \degree$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the other angle $= a$

As the triangle is an isosceles triangle, third angle $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + a + 30      &= 180 \\\\
2a + 30         &= 180 \\\\
2a              &= 180 - 30 \\\\
2a              &= 150 \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {150} {2} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {75 \times 2} {2} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {75 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
a               &= 75 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Other angle $= 75 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>E</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$75 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_angles_of_triangle
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the other angle $= a$
<emptyline>
As the triangle is an isosceles triangle, third angle $= a$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
a + a + 30      &= 180 \\\\
2a + 30         &= 180 \\\\
2a              &= 180 - 30 \\\\
2a              &= 150 \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {150} {2} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {75 \times 2} {2} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {75 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
a               &= 75 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Other angle $= 75 \degree$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">E</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Two of the angles in a triangle are $25 \degree$ and $107 \degree$. The third angle is:

-  $28 \degree$ 
-  $48 \degree$ 
-  $68 \degree$ 
-  $88 \degree$ 
-  $180 \degree$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Two of the angles in a triangle are $25 \degree$ and $107 \degree$. The third angle is:
<emptyline>
-  $28 \degree$ 
-  $48 \degree$ 
-  $68 \degree$ 
-  $88 \degree$ 
-  $180 \degree$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the third angle $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
25 + 107 + a &= 180 \\\\
132 + a      &= 180 \\\\
a            &= 180 - 132 \\\\
a            &= 48 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Third angle $= 48 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>B</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$48 \degree$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A right angled triangle has an angle of $55 \degree$.

What is the size of the smallest angle in the triangle?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A right angled triangle has an angle of $55 \degree$.
<emptyline>
What is the size of the smallest angle in the triangle?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the smallest angle $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 55 + 90     &= 180 \\\\
a + 145         &= 180 \\\\
a               &= 180 - 145 \\\\
a               &= 35 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Smallest angle $= 35 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$35 \degree$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A right angled triangle has an angle of $55 \degree$.

What is the size of the smallest angle in the triangle?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A right angled triangle has an angle of $55 \degree$.
<emptyline>
What is the size of the smallest angle in the triangle?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the smallest angle $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 55 + 90     &= 180 \\\\
a + 145         &= 180 \\\\
a               &= 180 - 145 \\\\
a               &= 35 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Smallest angle $= 35 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$35 \degree$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Calculate the angles labelled $a$ to $f$ in the diagrams below. The diagrams are **NOT DRAWN TO SCALE** :

![papers/benenden-school/2020/sample-paper-1/s01-q15-i00.png](/assets/papers/benenden-school/2020/sample-paper-1/s01-q15-i00.png "papers/benenden-school/2020/sample-paper-1/s01-q15-i00.png")
![papers/benenden-school/2020/sample-paper-1/s01-q15-i01.png](/assets/papers/benenden-school/2020/sample-paper-1/s01-q15-i01.png "papers/benenden-school/2020/sample-paper-1/s01-q15-i01.png")


</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Calculate the angles labelled $a$ to $f$ in the diagrams below. The diagrams are **NOT DRAWN TO SCALE** :
<emptyline>
%image%
%image%
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Angles on a straight line add up to} = 180 \degree$

- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the first diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 151                 &= 180 \\\\
a                       &= 180 - 151 \\\\
a                       &= 29 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Let's use the value of $a$ to find $b$.

$
\begin{aligned}
a + b       &= 180 \\\\
29 + b      &= 180 \\\\
b           &= 180 - 29 \\\\
b           &= 151 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

As per the second diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
c + 110 + 42    &= 180 \\\\
c + 152         &= 180 \\\\
c               &= 180 - 152 \\\\
c               &= 28 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

$c$ and $e$ are alternate angles,

$e = c = 28 \degree$

Similarly, 

$d = 42 \degree$

Now let's find angle $f$,

$
\begin{aligned}
d + e + f   &= 180 \\\\
42 + 28 + f &= 180 \\\\
70 + f      &= 180 \\\\
f           &= 180 - 70 \\\\
f           &= 110 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>a</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$29 \degree$

</div>
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>b</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$151 \degree$

</div>
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>c</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$28 \degree$

</div>
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>d</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$42 \degree$

</div>
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>e</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$28 \degree$

</div>
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>f</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$110 \degree$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the size, in degrees, of the angles labelled $a$ to $f$ in the diagrams below, which are NOT DRAWN TO SCALE:

![papers/benenden-school/2020/sample-paper-2/s01-q12-i00.png](/assets/papers/benenden-school/2020/sample-paper-2/s01-q12-i00.png "papers/benenden-school/2020/sample-paper-2/s01-q12-i00.png")
![papers/benenden-school/2020/sample-paper-2/s01-q12-i01.png](/assets/papers/benenden-school/2020/sample-paper-2/s01-q12-i01.png "papers/benenden-school/2020/sample-paper-2/s01-q12-i01.png")



</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the size, in degrees, of the angles labelled $a$ to $f$ in the diagrams below, which are NOT DRAWN TO SCALE:
<emptyline>
%image%
%image%
<emptyline>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Angles on a straight line add up to} = 180 \degree$

- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the first diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 62 \degree  &= 180 \\\\
a               &= 180 - 62 \\\\
a               &= 118 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

As per the first diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
b + 43 \degree  &= 180  \\\\
b               &= 180 - 43 \\\\
b               &= 137 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

As per the second diagram, angle $c$ is in isosceles triangle. So,

$c = 71 \degree$

Let's use the value of $c$ to find $d$.

As per the second diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
d + c + 71              &= 180   \\\\
d + 71 + 71             &= 180   \\\\
d + 142                 &= 180   \\\\
d                       &= 180  - 142  \\\\
d                       &= 38 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

$c$ and $e$ are vertically opposite angles. So,

$e = c = 71 \degree$

$71 \degree$ and $f$ are corresponding angles. So,

$f = 71 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>a</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$118 \degree$

</div>
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>b</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$137 \degree$

</div>
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>c</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$71 \degree$

</div>
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>d</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

</div>
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the sizes of angles $x$ and $y$.

![papers/brentwood-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s02-q03-i00.png](/assets/papers/brentwood-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s02-q03-i00.png "papers/brentwood-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s02-q03-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the sizes of angles $x$ and $y$.
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$

- ABBR: $\text{Angles on a straight line add up to} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
x + 104     &= 180   \\\\
x           &= 180  - 104  \\\\
x           &= 76 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Let's use the value of $x$ to find $y$.

$
\begin{aligned}
x  + y + y              &= 180 \\\\
76  + 2y                &= 180 \\\\
2y                      &= 180  - 76 \\\\
2y                      &= 104  \\\\
y                       &= \dfrac {104 } {2}   \\\\
y                       &= \dfrac {52 \times 2} {2}   \\\\
y                       &= \dfrac {52 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
y                       &= 52 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$x$ $=$ $76 \degree$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$y$ $=$ $52 \degree$

</div>
</div>

- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
x + 44 + 70     &= 180 \\\\
x + 114         &= 180 \\\\
x               &= 180 - 144 \\\\
x               &= 36 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

**Question**

![papers/city-of-london-freemens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s03-q03-i00.png](/assets/papers/city-of-london-freemens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s03-q03-i00.png "papers/city-of-london-freemens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s03-q03-i00.png")

-  $a = 58 \degree, \ b = 63 \degree$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many right angles are there in two complete turns?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many right angles are there in two complete turns?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{One complete turn}                    &= 360 \degree \\\\
\text{One right angle}                      &=90 \degree \\\\
\text {Right angles in two turns}           &= \dfrac {360 \times 2} {90} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {90 \times 4 \times 2} {90} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {\cancel {90} \times 4 \times 2} {\cancel {90}} \\\\
                                            &= 4 \times 2 \\\\
                                            &= 8
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$8$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{One complete turn}                    &= 360 \degree \\\\
\text{One right angle}                      &=90 \degree \\\\
\text {Right angles in two turns}           &= \dfrac {360 \times 2} {90} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {90 \times 4 \times 2} {90} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {\cancel {90} \times 4 \times 2} {\cancel {90}} \\\\
                                            &= 4 \times 2 \\\\
                                            &= 8
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

![papers/devonport-high-school-for-boys/2016/sample-paper-1/s01-q06-i00.png](/assets/papers/devonport-high-school-for-boys/2016/sample-paper-1/s01-q06-i00.png "papers/devonport-high-school-for-boys/2016/sample-paper-1/s01-q06-i00.png")

ABCDEF is a regular hexagon. What is the size of angle $x$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%image%
<emptyline>
ABCDEF is a regular hexagon. What is the size of angle $x$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Exterior Angle} = \dfrac{360 \degree} {\text{Number of sides}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
x           &= \dfrac {360} {6} \\\\
            &= \dfrac {60 \times 6} {6} \\\\
            &= \dfrac {60 \times 6} {6} \\\\
            &= \dfrac {60 \times \cancel{6}} {\cancel{6}} \\\\
            &= 60 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$60 \degree$

</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If two angles of a triangle add up to $137 \degree$, what is the size of the third angle?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If two angles of a triangle add up to $137 \degree$, what is the size of the third angle?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the size of the third angle $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 137      &= 180  \\\\
a            &= 180 - 137  \\\\
a            &= 43 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Third angle $= 43 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$43 \degree$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

![papers/devonport-high-school-for-boys/2017/sample-paper-2/s01-q06-i00.png](/assets/papers/devonport-high-school-for-boys/2017/sample-paper-2/s01-q06-i00.png "papers/devonport-high-school-for-boys/2017/sample-paper-2/s01-q06-i00.png")

ABCDEF is a regular hexagon. What is the size of angle $x$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%image%
<emptyline>
ABCDEF is a regular hexagon. What is the size of angle $x$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Interior Angle} = \dfrac{\text{(Sides - 2)} \times 180 \degree} {\text{Sides}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
x           &= \dfrac {(6 - 2) \times 180} {6} \\\\
            &= \dfrac {4 \times 180} {6} \\\\
            &= \dfrac {4 \times 30 \times 6} {6} \\\\
            &= \dfrac {4 \times 30 \times \cancel{6}} {\cancel{6}} \\\\
            &= 4 \times 30 \\\\
            &= 120 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$120 \degree$

</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If two angles of a triangle are $36 \degree$ and $22 \degree$, what is the size of the third angle?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If two angles of a triangle are $36 \degree$ and $22 \degree$, what is the size of the third angle?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the size of the third angle $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 36 + 22     &= 180  \\\\
a + 58          &= 180  \\\\
a               &= 180 - 58  \\\\
a               &= 122 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Third angle $= 122 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$122 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

![papers/devonport-high-school-for-boys/2019/sample-paper-4/s01-q06-i00.png](/assets/papers/devonport-high-school-for-boys/2019/sample-paper-4/s01-q06-i00.png "papers/devonport-high-school-for-boys/2019/sample-paper-4/s01-q06-i00.png")

ABCDEF is a regular hexagon. What is the size of angle $x$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%image%
<emptyline>
ABCDEF is a regular hexagon. What is the size of angle $x$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Interior Angle} = \dfrac{\text{(Sides - 2)} \times 180 \degree} {\text{Sides}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
x           &= \dfrac {(6 - 2) \times 180} {6} \\\\
            &= \dfrac {4 \times 180} {6} \\\\
            &= \dfrac {4 \times 30 \times 6} {6} \\\\
            &= \dfrac {4 \times 30 \times \cancel{6}} {\cancel{6}} \\\\
            &= 4 \times 30 \\\\
            &= 120 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$120 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_polygon_interior_angle
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
x           &= \dfrac {(6 - 2) \times 180} {6} \\\\
            &= \dfrac {4 \times 180} {6} \\\\
            &= \dfrac {4 \times 30 \times 6} {6} \\\\
            &= \dfrac {4 \times 30 \times \cancel{6}} {\cancel{6}} \\\\
            &= 4 \times 30 \\\\
            &= 120 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$120 \degree$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If two angles of a triangle add up to $89 \degree$, what is the size of the third angle?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If two angles of a triangle add up to $89 \degree$, what is the size of the third angle?
</code></pre>

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$

- ABBR: $\text{Angles on a straight line add up to} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the third angle of the triangle $= a$

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 70 &= 180 \\\\
a      &= 180 - 70 \\\\
a      &= 110 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Let's use the value of $a$ to find $y$.

$
\begin{aligned}
y + y + a   &= 180 \\\\
2y + 110    &= 180 \\\\
2y          &= 180 - 110 \\\\
2y          &= 70 \\\\
y           &= \dfrac {70} {2} \\\\
y           &= 35 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$35 \degree$

</div>
</div>

\begin{aligned}
y + y + 108 &= 180 \\\\
2y + 108    &= 180 \\\\
2y          &= 180 - 108 \\\\
2y          &= 72 \\\\
y           &= \dfrac {72} {2} \\\\
y           &= \dfrac {2 \times 36} {2} \\\\
y           &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 36} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
y           &= 36 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In the diagrams below, which angle is the biggest? The diagrams are not drawn to scale.
 
 ![papers/halifax-grammar-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q08-i00.png](/assets/papers/halifax-grammar-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q08-i00.png "papers/halifax-grammar-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q08-i00.png")
 ![papers/halifax-grammar-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q08-i01.png](/assets/papers/halifax-grammar-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q08-i01.png "papers/halifax-grammar-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q08-i01.png")
 ![papers/halifax-grammar-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q08-i02.png](/assets/papers/halifax-grammar-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q08-i02.png "papers/halifax-grammar-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q08-i02.png")
 ![papers/halifax-grammar-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q08-i03.png](/assets/papers/halifax-grammar-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q08-i03.png "papers/halifax-grammar-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q08-i03.png")

- $a$
- $b$
- $c$
- $d$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In the diagrams below, which angle is the biggest? The diagrams are not drawn to scale.
<emptyline>
 %image%
 %image%
 %image%
 %image%
<emptyline>
- $a$
- $b$
- $c$
- $d$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Angles on a straight line add up to} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 112             &= 180 \\\\
a                   &= 180 - 112 \\\\
a                   &= 68 \degree \\\\
b + 36 + 90         &= 180 \\\\
b                   &= 180 - 126 \\\\
b                   &= 54 \degree \\\\
c + 40 + 90         &= 180 \\\\
c                   &= 180 - 130 \\\\
c                   &= 50 \degree \\\\
2d + 70             &= 180 \\\\
2d                  &= 180 - 70 \\\\
d                   &= \dfrac{110} {2} \\\\
d                   &= \dfrac{55 \times 2} {2} \\\\
d                   &= \dfrac{55 \times \cancel{2}} {\cancel{2}} \\\\
d                   &= 55 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Biggest angle $a = 68 \degree$.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>A</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

a $=$ $68 \degree$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Here is a right angled triangle inside a rectangle. Calculate the value of angle $x$. Do not use a protractor.

![papers/john-lyon-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q21-i00.png](/assets/papers/john-lyon-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q21-i00.png "papers/john-lyon-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q21-i00.png") 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Here is a right angled triangle inside a rectangle. Calculate the value of angle $x$. Do not use a protractor.
<emptyline>
%image% 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$

- ABBR: $\text{Angles on a straight line add up to} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the third angle of the triangle $=a$

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
83 + a + 58  &= 180 \\\\
141 + a      &= 180 \\\\
a            &= 180 - 141 \\\\
a            &= 39 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Let's use the value of $a$ to find $x$.

$
\begin{aligned}
x + 39 + 90  &= 180 \\\\
x + 129      &= 180 \\\\
x            &= 180 - 129 \\\\
x            &= 51 \degree 
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$51 \degree$

</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$x \degree$
</code></pre>

<div class="workings">

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As the triangle is an isosceles triangle, the third angle is also $= x$

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
x + 40 + x  &= 180 \\\\
2 x + 40    &= 180 \\\\
2 x         &= 180 - 40 \\\\
2 x         &= 140 \\\\
x           &= \dfrac {140} {2} \\\\
x           &= \dfrac {70 \times 2} {2} \\\\
x           &= \dfrac {70 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
x           &= 70 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$70 \degree$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

One of the angles of an isosceles triangle is $96 \degree$. Find the sizes of the other two angles.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">One of the angles of an isosceles triangle is $96 \degree$. Find the sizes of the other two angles.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the missing angle $= a$

As the triangle is an isosceles triangle, the third angle is also $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + a + 96      &= 180  \\\\
2a + 96         &= 180  \\\\
2a              &= 180 - 96  \\\\
2a              &= 84  \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {84} {2}  \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {42 \times 2} {2}  \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {42 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}}   \\\\
a               &= 42 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Other two angles $= 42 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$42 \degree$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In a triangle, one of the angles is $45$ degrees. The other two angles in the triangle are in the ratio $4 : 5$. How big is the largest angle in the triangle? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In a triangle, one of the angles is $45$ degrees. The other two angles in the triangle are in the ratio $4 : 5$. How big is the largest angle in the triangle? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the second angle $= 4a$

Let the third angle $= 5a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
4a  + 5a + 45                    &= 180 \\\\
9a + 45                          &= 180 \\\\
9a                               &= 180 - 45 \\\\
9a                               &= 135 \\\\
a                                &= \dfrac {135} {9} \\\\
a                                &= \dfrac {9 \times 15} {9} \\\\
a                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {9} \times 15} {\cancel {9}} \\\\
a                                &= 15 \degree \\\\
\text{Biggest angle}             &= 5a \\\\
                                 &= 5 \times 15 \\\\
                                 &= 75 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$75 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_angles_of_triangle
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the second angle $= 4a$
<emptyline>
Let the third angle $= 5a$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}

9a                               &= 135 \\\\

a                                &= \dfrac {9 \times 15} {9} \\\\
a                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {9} \times 15} {\cancel {9}} \\\\
a                                &= 15 \degree \\\\
\text{Biggest angle}             &= 5a \\\\
                                 &= 5 \times 15 \\\\
                                 &= 75 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

An equilateral triangle has three sides the same length. What is the size of one of the angles? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">An equilateral triangle has three sides the same length. What is the size of one of the angles? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: $\text{All angles of an equilateral triangle are equal}$.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$

- ABBR: $\text{Each angle of an equilateral triangle} = 60 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Angle $= 60 \degree$

We can also calculate it as below:

Let the angle $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + a + a              &= 180 \degree \\\\
3a                     &= 180 \\\\
a                      &= \dfrac {180} {3} \\\\
a                      &= \dfrac {3 \times 60} {3} \\\\
a                      &= \dfrac {\cancel{3} \times 60} {\cancel{3}} \\\\
a                      &= 60 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Angle $= 60 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$60 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_angles_of_an_equilateral_triangle
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_angles_of_triangle
- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_angles_of_equilateral_triangle
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Angle $= 60 \degree$
<emptyline>
We can also calculate it as below:
<emptyline>
Let the angle $= a$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
a + a + a              &= 180 \degree \\\\
3a                     &= 180 \\\\
a                      &= \dfrac {180} {3} \\\\
a                      &= \dfrac {3 \times 60} {3} \\\\
a                      &= \dfrac {\cancel{3} \times 60} {\cancel{3}} \\\\
a                      &= 60 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Angle $= 60 \degree$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$60 \degree$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Look at the triangle.

![papers/sevenoaks-school/2010/sample-paper-1/s01-q16-i00.png](/assets/papers/sevenoaks-school/2010/sample-paper-1/s01-q16-i00.png "papers/sevenoaks-school/2010/sample-paper-1/s01-q16-i00.png")

Work out the value of $a$ and $b$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>

<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
Work out the value of $a$ and $b$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing starts.*

{/* ![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg) */}
TODOIMAGE



*TODO:: Add an image with vertices marked ABCD$*


*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing ends.*


In triangle $ABD$, as per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
2a + a + 90     &= 180 \\\\
3a + 90         &= 180 \\\\
3a              &= 180 - 90 \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {90} {3} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {30 \times 3} {3} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {30 \times \cancel{3}} { \cancel{3}} \\\\
a               &= 30 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Let's use value of $a$ in triangle ABC.

$
\begin{aligned}
(b + 2a) + b + 90     	&= 180 \\\\
2b + 2a             	&= 180 - 90 \\\\
2b + 2 \times 30     	&= 90 \\\\
2b                  	&= 90 - 60 \\\\
b                   	&= \dfrac {30} {2} \\\\
b                   	&= \dfrac {15 \times 2} {2} \\\\
b                   	&= \dfrac {15 \times \cancel {2} } {\cancel {2} } \\\\
b                   	&= 15 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$a$ $=$ $30 \degree$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$b$ $=$ $15 \degree$

</div>
</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
2x + x + 5x + 4x                &= 360 \\\\
12x                             &= 360 \\\\
x                               &= \dfrac {360} {12} \\\\
x                               &= \dfrac {30 \times 12} {12} \\\\
x                               &= \dfrac {30 \times \cancel {12}} {\cancel {12}} \\\\
x                               &= 30 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$30 \degree$

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
2x + (x + 32) + 40 			&= 180 \degree \\\\
2x + x + 32 + 40            &= 180 \\\\
3x + 72                     &= 180 \\\\
3x                          &= 180 - 72 \\\\
3x                          &= 108 \\\\
x                           &= \dfrac {108} {3} \\\\
x                           &= \dfrac {36 \times 3} {3} \\\\
x                           &= \dfrac {36 \times \cancel {3}} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
x                           &= 36 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$36 \degree$

</div>
</div>

c + 75  &= 180 \\\\
c       &= 180 - 75 \\\\
c       &= 105 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

\end{aligned}

</div>
<div class="papername">
<p>st-albans-high-school-for-girls--11-plus--maths--2016--sample-paper-1:1:24</p>

<div class="rag">

<div class="rag">

<div class="rag">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

![papers/st-albans-high-school-for-girls/2016/sample-paper-1/s01-q24-i00.png](/assets/papers/st-albans-high-school-for-girls/2016/sample-paper-1/s01-q24-i00.png "papers/st-albans-high-school-for-girls/2016/sample-paper-1/s01-q24-i00.png") 

The angles in a triangle always add up to $180 \degree$ . In the triangle shown above angle $a \degree$ is three times as big as angle $b \degree$ and angle $b \degree$ is twice as large as angle $c \degree$. Find the angle $d \degree$ shown in the diagram.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%image% 
<emptyline>
The angles in a triangle always add up to $180 \degree$ . In the triangle shown above angle $a \degree$ is three times as big as angle $b \degree$ and angle $b \degree$ is twice as large as angle $c \degree$. Find the angle $d \degree$ shown in the diagram.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$

- ABBR: $\text{Angles on a straight line add up to} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
b                                             &= 2c \\\\
a                                             &= 3b \\\\
a                                             &= 3 \times 2c \\\\
a + b + c                                     &= 180 \\\\
3 \times 2c+ 2c + c                           &= 180 \\\\
6c+ 2c + c                                    &= 180 \\\\
9c                                            &= 180 \\\\
c                                             &= \dfrac {180} {9} \\\\
c                                             &= 20 \degree \\\\
b                                             &= 2c \\\\
b                                             &= 2 \times 20 \\\\
b                                             &= 40 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Let's use the value of $b$ to find $d$.

$
\begin{aligned}
b + d                                         &= 180 \\\\
40 + d                                        &= 180 \\\\
d                                             &= 180 - 40 \\\\
d                                             &= 140 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$140 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_angles_of_triangle
- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_angles_on_a_straight_line
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
b                                             &= 2c \\\\
a                                             &= 3b \\\\
a                                             &= 3 \times 2c \\\\
a + b + c                                     &= 180 \\\\
3 \times 2c+ 2c + c                           &= 180 \\\\
6c+ 2c + c                                    &= 180 \\\\
9c                                            &= 180 \\\\
c                                             &= \dfrac {180} {9} \\\\
c                                             &= 20 \degree \\\\
b                                             &= 2c \\\\
b                                             &= 2 \times 20 \\\\
b                                             &= 40 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Let's use the value of $b$ to find $d$.
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
b + d                                         &= 180 \\\\
40 + d                                        &= 180 \\\\
d                                             &= 180 - 40 \\\\
d                                             &= 140 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$140 \degree$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The diagram shows two equilateral triangles and a rectangle. Calculate the size of angle $a \degree$.

![papers/st-albans-high-school-for-girls/2016/sample-paper-1/s01-q27-i00.png](/assets/papers/st-albans-high-school-for-girls/2016/sample-paper-1/s01-q27-i00.png "papers/st-albans-high-school-for-girls/2016/sample-paper-1/s01-q27-i00.png") 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The diagram shows two equilateral triangles and a rectangle. Calculate the size of angle $a \degree$.
<emptyline>
%image% 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Angles around a point add up to} = 360 \degree$

- ABBR: $\text{Each angle of an equilateral triangle} = 60 \degree$

- ABBR: $\text{Each angle of a rectangle} = 90 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
90 + 60 + 60 + a                               &= 360 \\\\
a                                              &= 360 - 90 - 60 - 60\\\\
a                                              &= 150 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$150 \degree$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Two angles in a triangle are $30 \degree$ and $40 \degree$. Find the third angle. 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Two angles in a triangle are $30 \degree$ and $40 \degree$. Find the third angle. 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the third angle $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 30 + 40     &= 180 \\\\ 
a + 70          &= 180 \\\\ 
a               &= 180 - 70 \\\\ 
a               &= 110 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Third angle $= 110 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$110 \degree$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Two of the angles in a quardilateral are right angles. One of the others is five times larger than the fourth angle.

What is the size of the largest angle?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Two of the angles in a quardilateral are right angles. One of the others is five times larger than the fourth angle.
<emptyline>
What is the size of the largest angle?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a quadrilateral} = 360 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the third angle $= a$

Fourth angle $= 5a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
90 + 90 + a + 5a            &= 360 \\\\
180 + 6a                    &= 360 \\\\
6a                          &= 360 - 180 \\\\
6a                          &= 180 \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {180} {6} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {6 \times 30} {6} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {\cancel {6} \times 30} {\cancel {6}} \\\\
a                           &= 30 \degree \\\\
\text {Largest angle}       &= 5a \\\\
                            &= 5 \times 30 \\\\ 
                            &= 150 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$150\degree$

</div>
