<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In this  triangle angle $A$  is the   same  as angle $B$, and   angle $C$  is twice angle $B$. Work  out   each  of the   three angles.

![papers/st-albans-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q21-s01-i00.png](/assets/papers/st-albans-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q21-s01-i00.png "papers/st-albans-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q21-s01-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In this  triangle angle $A$  is the   same  as angle $B$, and   angle $C$  is twice angle $B$. Work  out   each  of the   three angles.
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
A                        &= B \\\\
C                        &= 2B \\\\
A + B + C                &= 180 \\\\
B + B + 2B               &= 180 \\\\
4B                       &= 180 \\\\
B                        &= \dfrac {180} {4} \\\\
B                        &= \dfrac {45 \times 4} {4} \\\\
B                        &= \dfrac {45 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
B                        &= 45 \degree \\\\
A                        &= 45 \degree \\\\
C                        &= 2B \\\\
C                        &= 2 \times 45 \\\\
C                        &= 90 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$A$ $=$ $45 \degree$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$B$ $=$ $45 \degree$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$C$ $=$ $90 \degree$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The   angles   round a  point add   up to $360 \degree$

In the   diagram  below,   one   angle is $90 \degree$ and the   other three are   equal to each  other.   Work  out   the   missing  angles.

![papers/st-albans-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q21-s02-i00.png](/assets/papers/st-albans-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q21-s02-i00.png "papers/st-albans-school/sample/sample-paper-2/s01-q21-s02-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The   angles   round a  point add   up to $360 \degree$
<emptyline>
In the   diagram  below,   one   angle is $90 \degree$ and the   other three are   equal to each  other.   Work  out   the   missing  angles.
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Angles around a point add up to} = 360 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
x + x + x + 90  &= 360 \\\\
3x              &= 360 - 90 \\\\
3x              &= 270 \\\\
x               &= \dfrac {270} {3} \\\\
x               &= \dfrac {3 \times 90} {3} \\\\
x               &= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 90} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
x               &= 90 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$90 \degree$

</div>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
x + x + x + 90  &= 360 \\\\
3x              &= 360 - 90 \\\\
3x              &= 270 \\\\
x               &= \dfrac {270} {3} \\\\
x               &= \dfrac {3 \times 90} {3} \\\\
x               &= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 90} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
x               &= 90 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$90 \degree$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>

- ABBR: $\text{Angles on a straight line add up to} = 180 \degree$
</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
62 + 45 + f &= 180 \\\\
f + 107     &= 180 \\\\
f           &= 180 - 107 \\\\
f           &= 73 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Let's use the value of $f$ to find $g$.

$
\begin{aligned}
f + g     &=  180 \\\\
73 + g    &=  180 \\\\
g         &=  180 - 73 \\\\
g         &=  107 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$f$ $=$ $73 \degree$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$g$ $=$ $107 \degree$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Calculate the angle marked with the letter $x$.

![papers/streatham-and-clapham-high-school/2019/sample-paper-2/s01-q03-i00.png](/assets/papers/streatham-and-clapham-high-school/2019/sample-paper-2/s01-q03-i00.png "papers/streatham-and-clapham-high-school/2019/sample-paper-2/s01-q03-i00.png")

- $70 \degree$
- $85 \degree$
- $90 \degree$
- $75 \degree$
- $60 \degree$ 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>

<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
- $70 \degree$
- $85 \degree$
- $90 \degree$
- $75 \degree$
- $60 \degree$ 
</code></pre>

$
\begin{aligned}
x + 43 + 52     &= 180  \\\\
x + 95          &= 180  \\\\
x               &= 180 - 95  \\\\
x               &= 85 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Two angles of a quadrilateral are $95 \degree$ and $75 \degree$. 
Which of the following could be the difference of the other two angles?

- $190$
- $180$
- $195$
- $200$
- $205$ 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Two angles of a quadrilateral are $95 \degree$ and $75 \degree$. 
Which of the following could be the difference of the other two angles?
<emptyline>
- $190$
- $180$
- $195$
- $200$
- $205$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a quadrilateral} = 360 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

*TODO:: Problem statement should be corrected, sum of angles instead of difference.*

Let the sum of other two angles be $= s$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
s + 95 + 75 &= 360 \\\\
s + 170     &= 360 \\\\
s           &= 360 - 170 \\\\
s           &= 190 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Sum of the other two angles $= 190 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>A</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$190 \degree$

</div>
</div>

</div>

y           &= 30 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The diagram shows five and six sided polygons in which all sides have the same length.

Complete the table below.

The following facts may be useful:

- the angle in one complete turn is $360 \degree$   
- the angles in a triangle add up to $180 \degree$ 

<br />

|  	| Five Sided Polygon 	| Six Sided Polygon 	|
|:---:	|:---:	|:---:	|
| Name of shape 	|  	|  	|
| Size of angle $a$ 	|  	|  	|
| Size of angle $b$ 	|  	|  	|
| Name of triangle $ABC$ 	|  	|  	|

![papers/the-haberdashers-askes-boys-school/2013/sample-paper-4/s01-q26-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-haberdashers-askes-boys-school/2013/sample-paper-4/s01-q26-i00.png "papers/the-haberdashers-askes-boys-school/2013/sample-paper-4/s01-q26-i00.png")

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

One angle of an isosceles triangle is $80 \degree$. 

What are the other angles? 

There are two possible solutions to this question.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">One angle of an isosceles triangle is $80 \degree$. 
<emptyline>
What are the other angles? 
<emptyline>
There are two possible solutions to this question.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

This can be solved two ways.

**OPTION 1**

Let the two equal angles of the isosceles triangle $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a  + a + 80                             &= 180 \\\\
2a + 80                                 &= 180 \\\\
2a                                      &= 180 - 80 \\\\
2a                                      &= 100 \\\\
a                                       &= \dfrac {100} {2} \\\\
a                                       &= \dfrac {50 \times 2} {2} \\\\
a                                       &= \dfrac {50 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a                                       &= 50 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Second angle $= 50 \degree$

Third angle $= 50 \degree$

**OPTION 2**

Let the third angle $= b$

Let the two equal angles of the isosceles triangle $= 80 \degree$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
b  + 80 + 80                            &= 180 \\\\
b + 160                                 &= 180 \\\\
b                                       &= 180 - 160 \\\\
b                                       &= 20 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Second angle $= 20 \degree$

Third angle $= 80 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$50 \degree, 50 \degree$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$20 \degree, 80 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_angles_of_triangle
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

a                                       &= 50 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Second angle $= 50 \degree$
<emptyline>
Third angle $= 50 \degree$
<emptyline>
**OPTION 2**
<emptyline>

<emptyline>

<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Jake hangs a peg basket on a washing line.

![papers/the-independent-schools-examinations-board/2016/sample-paper-5/s01-q18-s01-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-independent-schools-examinations-board/2016/sample-paper-5/s01-q18-s01-i00.png "papers/the-independent-schools-examinations-board/2016/sample-paper-5/s01-q18-s01-i00.png")

Find the size of one of the shaded angles in the diagram above, if both are the same size.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Jake hangs a peg basket on a washing line.
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
Find the size of one of the shaded angles in the diagram above, if both are the same size.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Angles around a point add up to} = 360 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the angle $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + a + 140     &= 360 \\\\
2a              &= 220 \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {220} {2} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {2 \times 110} {2} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 110} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a               &= 110 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$110 \degree$

</div>
</div>

![papers/the-independent-schools-examinations-board/2016/sample-paper-5/s01-q18-s02-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-independent-schools-examinations-board/2016/sample-paper-5/s01-q18-s02-i00.png "papers/the-independent-schools-examinations-board/2016/sample-paper-5/s01-q18-s02-i00.png")

%image%
</code></pre>

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Angles on a straight line add up to} = 180 \degree$

- ABBR: $\text{Vertically opposite angles are equal}$.
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
a       &= 30 \degree \\\\
b + 30  &= 180 \\\\
b       &= 180 - 30 \\\\
b       &= 150 \degree \\\\
c       &= b \\\\
        &= 150 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

a $=$ $30 \degree$

![papers/the-kings-school-chester/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q18-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-kings-school-chester/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q18-i00.png "papers/the-kings-school-chester/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q18-i00.png")

<div class="workings">

- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$

- ABBR: $\text{Angles on a straight line add up to} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the third angle in the triangle $= y$

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
y + 36 + 42     &= 180 \\\\
y + 78          &= 180 \\\\
y               &= 180 - 78 \\\\
y               &= 102 \degree \\\\
x + y           &= 180 \\\\
x + 102         &= 180 \\\\
x               &= 180 - 102 \\\\
x               &= 78 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$78 \degree$

</div>

</div>

$
\begin{aligned}
a  + 40 + 60                            &= 180 \\\\

a                                       &= 80 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

$x$ is vertically opposite to $a$ and equal to $a$.

$x = 80 \degree$
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The shape is made from a square and an equilateral triangle.

![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2010/group-1/s01-q29-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2010/group-1/s01-q29-i00.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2010/group-1/s01-q29-i00.png")

Diagram not drawn to scale.

What is the size of the reflex angle marked $p \degree$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The shape is made from a square and an equilateral triangle.
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
Diagram not drawn to scale.
<emptyline>
What is the size of the reflex angle marked $p \degree$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Each angle of an equilateral triangle} = 60 \degree$

- ABBR: $\text{Each angle of a square} = 90 \degree$

- ABBR: $\text{Angles around a point add up to} = 360 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
p + 60 + 90  &= 360 \\\\
p + 150      &= 360 \\\\
p            &= 360 - 150 \\\\
p            &= 210 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$210 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

One angle of a triangle is $30 \degree$. The second angle is twice the size of the third. What is the largest angle?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">One angle of a triangle is $30 \degree$. The second angle is twice the size of the third. What is the largest angle?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the second angle $= a$

Third angle $= 2a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 2a + 30     &= 180  \\\\
a + 2a + 30     &= 180  \\\\
3a + 30         &= 180  \\\\
3a              &= 180 - 30  \\\\
3a              &= 150  \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {150} {3}  \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {50 \times 3} {3}  \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {50 \times \cancel{3}}{\cancel{3}}   \\\\
a               &= 50 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Second angle}    &= 50 \degree \\\\
\text {Third angle}     &= 2a \\\\
                        &= 2 \times 50 \\\\
                        &= 100 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Largest angle $= 100 \degree$ 
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$100 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_angles_of_triangle
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the second angle $= a$
<emptyline>
Third angle $= 2a$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
a + 2a + 30     &= 180  \\\\
a + 2a + 30     &= 180  \\\\
3a + 30         &= 180  \\\\
3a              &= 180 - 30  \\\\
3a              &= 150  \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {150} {3}  \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {50 \times 3} {3}  \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {50 \times \cancel{3}}{\cancel{3}}   \\\\
a               &= 50 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Second angle}    &= 50 \degree \\\\
\text {Third angle}     &= 2a \\\\
                        &= 2 \times 50 \\\\
                        &= 100 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Largest angle $= 100 \degree$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The formula to work out the sum of the angles inside any shape is 

$\text{Sum of angles} = 180 \degree \times \text{number of sides} - 360 \degree$

For example,

![papers/the-manchester-grammar-school/2011/arithmetic-2/s01-q04-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-manchester-grammar-school/2011/arithmetic-2/s01-q04-i00.png "papers/the-manchester-grammar-school/2011/arithmetic-2/s01-q04-i00.png")

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text{a} + \text{b} + \text{c} + \text{d} + \text{e} &= 180 \degree \times 5 - 360 \degree \\
                  &= 900 \degree - 360 \degree \\
                  &= 540 \degree
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$

**Question**

Find $\text{a}$.

![papers/the-manchester-grammar-school/2011/arithmetic-2/s01-q04-s01-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-manchester-grammar-school/2011/arithmetic-2/s01-q04-s01-i00.png "papers/the-manchester-grammar-school/2011/arithmetic-2/s01-q04-s01-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find $\text{a}$.
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question, 

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 120 + 70 + 60                                     &= 180 \times 4 - 360 \\\\
a + 250                                               &= 720 - 360 \\\\
a + 250                                               &= 360 \\\\
a                                                     &= 360 - 250 \\\\
a                                                     &= 110 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$110 \degree$

</div>
</div>

<div class="workings">

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question, 

$
\begin{aligned}
b + b + b + 150 + 120 + 60                                                     &= 180 \times 6 - 360 \\\\
3b + 330                                                                       &= 1080 - 360 \\\\
3b + 330                                                                       &= 720 \\\\
3b                                                                             &= 720 - 330 \\\\
3b                                                                             &= 360 \\\\
b                                                                              &= \dfrac {390} {3} \\\\
b                                                                              &= \dfrac {130 \times 3} {3} \\\\
b                                                                              &= \dfrac {130 \times \cancel{3}} {\cancel{3}} \\\\
b                                                                              &= 130 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find $\text{c}$.

![papers/the-manchester-grammar-school/2011/arithmetic-2/s01-q04-s03-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-manchester-grammar-school/2011/arithmetic-2/s01-q04-s03-i00.png "papers/the-manchester-grammar-school/2011/arithmetic-2/s01-q04-s03-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find $\text{c}$.
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Angles on a straight line add up to} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question, 

$
\begin{aligned}
8x                                          &= 180 \times 8 - 360 \\\\
8x                                          &= 1440 - 360 \\\\
8x                                          &= 1080 \\\\
x                                           &= \dfrac {1080} {8} \\\\
x                                           &= \dfrac {135 \times 8} {8} \\\\
x                                           &= \dfrac {135 \times \cancel{8}} {\cancel{8}} \\\\
x                                           &= 135 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Let's use the value of $x$ to find $c$.

$
\begin{aligned}
x + c                        &= 180 \\\\
135 + c                      &= 180 \\\\
c                            &= 180 - 135 \\\\
c                            &= 45 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$45 \degree$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In a triangle, two angles are the same and the third angle is THREE times bigger than either of the others.  What is the size of the biggest angle?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In a triangle, two angles are the same and the third angle is THREE times bigger than either of the others.  What is the size of the biggest angle?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the two equal angles $= a$

Third angle $= 3a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + a + 3a      &= 180 \degree \\\\
5a              &= 180 \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {180} {5} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {36 \times 5} {5} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {36 \times \cancel{5}} {\cancel{5}} \\\\
a               &= 36 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Biggest angle}   &= 3a \\\\
                        &= 3 \times 36 \\\\
                        &= 108 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$108 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">

</div>

<emptyline>

<emptyline>

<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}

5a              &= 180 \\\\

a               &= \dfrac {36 \times 5} {5} \\\\
a               &= \dfrac {36 \times \cancel{5}} {\cancel{5}} \\\\
a               &= 36 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>

</code></pre>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In a triangle, one of the angles is $39\degree$, the second is $81\degree$, what is the size of the third angle?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In a triangle, one of the angles is $39\degree$, the second is $81\degree$, what is the size of the third angle?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the third angle $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 39 + 81   &= 180 \\\\
a + 120       &= 180 \\\\
a             &= 180 - 120 \\\\
a             &= 60 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Third angle $= 60 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$60 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A second triangle is isosceles, and one of its angles is $48\degree$. What are the TWO possible pairs of values for the other two angles?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A second triangle is isosceles, and one of its angles is $48\degree$. What are the TWO possible pairs of values for the other two angles?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

This can be solved two ways.

**OPTION 1**

Let the two equal angles of the isosceles triangle $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + a + 48           &= 180 \\\\
2a + 48              &= 180 \\\\
2a                   &= 180 - 48 \\\\
2a                   &= 132 \\\\
a                    &= \dfrac {132} {2} \\\\
a                    &= 66 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Other two angles $= 66 \degree , 66 \degree$

**OPTION 2**

Let the third angle $= b$

Let the two equal angles of the isosceles triangle $= 48 \degree$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
48 + 48 + b    &= 180 \\\\
96 + b         &= 180 \\\\
b              &= 180 - 96 \\\\
b              &= 84 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

Other two angles $= 48 \degree, 84 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$66 \degree, 66 \degree$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$48 \degree, 84 \degree$

</div>
</div>

</div>

\end{aligned}

</code></pre>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In a third triangle $\text{ABC}$, angle $B$ is two times the size of angle $A$ and angle $C$ is $40\degree$ more than angle $A$. 
What is the size of angle $A?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In a third triangle $\text{ABC}$, angle $B$ is two times the size of angle $A$ and angle $C$ is $40\degree$ more than angle $A$. 
What is the size of angle $A?$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
A + B + C               &= 180 \\\\
A + (2A) + (40 + A)     &= 180 \\\\
4A + 40                 &= 180 \\\\
4A                      &= 180 - 40 \\\\
A                       &= \dfrac {160} {4} \\\\
A                       &= \dfrac {4 \times 40} {4} \\\\
A                       &= \dfrac {\cancel {4} \times 40} {\cancel {4} } \\\\
A                       &= 40 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$40 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_angles_of_triangle
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

<emptyline>
$

\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

One of the angles in a triangle is $40 \degree$. One of the remaining two angles is three times larger than the other angle. What is the largest angle in the triangle?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">One of the angles in a triangle is $40 \degree$. One of the remaining two angles is three times larger than the other angle. What is the largest angle in the triangle?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the first angle $= a$

Second angle $= 3a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 3a + 40             &= 180 \\\\
4a + 40                 &= 180  \\\\
4a                      &= 180 - 40  \\\\
4a                      &= 140  \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {140} {4}  \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {35 \times 4} {4}  \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {35 \times \cancel{4}}{\cancel{4}}   \\\\
a                       &= 35 \degree \\\\
\text {Largest angle}   &= 3a  \\\\
                        &= 3 \times 35  \\\\
                        &= 105  \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$105 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">

</div>

a                       &= \dfrac {35 \times 4} {4}  \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {35 \times \cancel{4}}{\cancel{4}}   \\\\
a                       &= 35 \degree \\\\
\text {Largest angle}   &= 3a  \\\\
                        &= 3 \times 35  \\\\
                        &= 105  \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In a triangle, the largest angle is two times the middle angle and the middle angle is three times the smallest angle. What is the size of the **largest** angle?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In a triangle, the largest angle is two times the middle angle and the middle angle is three times the smallest angle. What is the size of the **largest** angle?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the smallest angle $= a$

Middle angle $= 3a$

Largest angle $= 6a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 3a + 6a             &= 180 \\\\
10a                     &= 180 \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {180} {10} \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {18\cancel{0}} {1\cancel{0}} \\\\
a                       &= 18 \degree \\\\
\text {Largest angle}   &= 6a \\\\
                        &= 6 \times 18 \\\\
                        &= 108 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$108 \degree$

</div>
</div>

\end{aligned}

x               &= 90 - 74 \\\\
x              &= 16 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
x + 60 + 14     &= 90 \\\\
x + 74          &= 90 \\\\
x               &= 90 - 74 \\\\
x              &= 16 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The triangle in the diagram below is isosceles. 

Calculate angle $x$

![papers/warwick-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q18-i00.png](/assets/papers/warwick-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q18-i00.png "papers/warwick-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q18-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The triangle in the diagram below is isosceles. 
<emptyline>
Calculate angle $x$
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$

- ABBR: $\text{Angles on a straight line add up to} = 180 \degree$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the third angle in the triangle $= a$

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 134                 &= 180 \\\\
a                       &= 180 - 134 \\\\
a                       &= 46 \degree \\\\
x + x + a               &= 180 \\\\
2x + 46                 &= 180 \\\\
2x                      &= 180 - 46 \\\\
2x                      &= 134 \\\\
x                       &= \dfrac{134} {2} \\\\
x                       &= \dfrac{2 \times 67} {2} \\\\
x                       &= \dfrac{\cancel {2} \times 67} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
x                       &= 67 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$67 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_angles_of_triangle
- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_angles_on_a_straight_line
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the third angle in the triangle $= a$
<emptyline>
As per the diagram,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
a + 134                 &= 180 \\\\
a                       &= 180 - 134 \\\\
a                       &= 46 \degree \\\\
x + x + a               &= 180 \\\\
2x + 46                 &= 180 \\\\
2x                      &= 180 - 46 \\\\
2x                      &= 134 \\\\
x                       &= \dfrac{134} {2} \\\\
x                       &= \dfrac{2 \times 67} {2} \\\\
x                       &= \dfrac{\cancel {2} \times 67} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
x                       &= 67 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
